# WP-51 library audit: jsonschema/v6 v6.0.3 under draft 2020-12

This is the complete inventory of what the JSON Schema library acts on when WP-51 (the validation.json-schema Policy, internal/filter/validation/jsonschema) compiles an operator's schema and validates a request body with it, and of how WP-51 bounds each item. It replaces the one-finding-at-a-time discovery of review rounds 1 to 9. Section 4 is the work list for the fix pass: every partial or gap row belongs to exactly one gap class, and each class has one fix and its tests.

Produced on 2026-10-16 by the resumed audit run of `.claude/plans/m1/m1-wp51-audit.js` (merge step), from the verified rows of run wf_eb3b9ef7-7cd (compile, validate, refs) and of this run (values, cross), finalized with the completeness check of this run (the CC rows; section 6). No code was changed by the audit.

## 1. Method and scope

### 1.1 What was audited

- Library: github.com/santhosh-tekuri/jsonschema/v6 v6.0.3, the version go.mod pins (source: /root/go/pkg/mod/github.com/santhosh-tekuri/jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3). All non-test files: compiler.go, content.go, draft.go, format.go, loader.go, objcompiler.go, output.go, position.go, root.go, roots.go, schema.go, util.go, validator.go, vocab.go, kind/kind.go, metaschemas/. Library citations are file:line in that tree; "schema.go (lib)" marks the library's schema.go where it could be confused with WP-51's.
- Go regexp and regexp/syntax as the library and WP-51 use them, read in go1.27.1 (the go.mod toolchain); the Go 1.26.8 floor was compared by source only.
- Code under audit: internal/filter/validation/jsonschema at develop 5f426d5 plus the post-fix8 changes (runs/wp51-postfix8.patch), that is the package tree of branch wip/wp51-audit-paused at af41491. WP-51 citations (walk.go, schema.go, working.go and so on) are file:line in that tree.
- Binding contract: spec 07 (specs/07-transform.md) reqs 74 to 84 and 90 and section 9 items 21 to 24; architecture R-63 and R-77 (specs/00-architecture.md); user decisions 12 to 15 (user-decisions.md, also the "decisions" field of runs/w3-s2-wp51-r9.json) and decision 17 of 2026-10-12: no restricted profile. Every keyword and mechanism the library acts on under draft 2020-12 must be bounded and tested, including the older-draft keywords its cumulative compile layers still enforce (draft 4, 6, 7 and 2019-09: for example dependencies, $recursiveRef and $recursiveAnchor).

### 1.2 How the rows were produced

The library was split into five areas. An auditor wrote the rows of each area; a verifier then checked every row adversarially, reclassified rows it could not prove, and added the rows the auditor missed. Only verified rows appear here; the auditor rows (results["audit:*"] in runs/w3-s2-wp51-audit-partial.json) are superseded. A completeness check then mapped every library function, every keyword string of objcompiler.go and draft.go and every Go regexp entry point to the rows, added six CC rows for what no row covered, and its evidence reclassified five rows (section 6).

| Area | Library files | Rows | Verified in |
|---|---|---|---|
| Compile (CMP) | objcompiler.go, draft.go, compiler.go, vocab.go, content.go, metaschemas/ | 79 | wf_eb3b9ef7-7cd |
| Validate (VAL) | validator.go | 69 | wf_eb3b9ef7-7cd |
| References and resources (REFS) | roots.go, root.go, loader.go, position.go, util.go (URL, pointer and escaping helpers) | 53 | wf_eb3b9ef7-7cd |
| Values (VLU) | kind/kind.go, util.go (equality, hashing, numbers), output.go, format.go | 56 | this run |
| Cross (regexp and interactions) (CR, CX) | Go regexp and regexp/syntax as the library uses them, plus interactions across all files | 58 | this run |
| Completeness check (CC) | every function of the non-test files and kind/kind.go, the keyword strings of objcompiler.go and draft.go, the Go regexp entry points | 6 | this run (completeness check) |

Verification used three kinds of evidence, named in each row:

- mutants: a copy of a WP-51 file with one bound removed or changed, reached with go test -overlay, run against the whole CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite. A "killed" mutant proves that a named test fails without the bound; a "survived" mutant means no test proves it;
- probes: scratch tests reached with go test -overlay and built with -o into a scratch directory, measuring time, allocation or kept heap for hostile inputs;
- code reading, where the cost cannot arise or follows from the cited lines.

Scratch locations named in the rows: `<v>` and `<scratch>` (compile and refs verifiers of run wf_eb3b9ef7-7cd) are in /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-ruralz/0aabfe6e-34a6-51d2-9b9b-23886b703760/scratchpad/wp51audit/, which no longer exists, so those probes must be rewritten from the shapes the rows give. The values verifier's files are in /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-ruralz/758d7ca3-81cf-5fbc-a967-12d97bce3d6b/scratchpad/wp51audit/verify-values/ (zz_vv_test.go to zz_vv5_test.go, mut/), and `<D>` in the cross rows is /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-ruralz/758d7ca3-81cf-5fbc-a967-12d97bce3d6b/scratchpad/wp51audit/verify-cross/ (zz_vc_*_test.go, mutate.py, muts*.json and .out). The completeness check's files are in /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-ruralz/758d7ca3-81cf-5fbc-a967-12d97bce3d6b/scratchpad/wp51audit/complete/ (zz_cc_*_test.go, overlay*.json, probe*.test built with go1.27.1; cover.py, cover.txt and funcs.txt map the library functions to rows). Timings were taken on a shared 4-CPU container at the load stated in each row; allocation figures do not depend on load.

### 1.3 Row identifiers

CMP (compile), VAL (validate), REFS (references and resources), VLU (values), CR (cross: Go regexp), CX (cross: interactions) and CC (completeness check). The values verifier numbered its rows VAL-01 to VAL-56, which collide with the validate area's VAL-01 to VAL-69; they are renamed VLU-01 to VLU-56 here, and every reference inside the values rows is renamed with them. Gap classes are GC-01 to GC-21 (section 4).

### 1.4 Cost dimensions

| Code | Meaning |
|---|---|
| C1 | compile CPU |
| C2 | compile peak memory |
| C3 | memory the compiled Schema keeps |
| V1 | validation CPU (decision and location passes) |
| V2 | validation memory (working set, error trees, transient buffers) |
| P | panic, crash, hang or unbounded recursion |
| W | wrong result (invalid accepted, valid refused, wrong keyword location or detail) |
| S | security (file or network access, loader, URL handling) |
| D | nondeterminism |

### 1.5 Status and severity

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| bounded | a WP-51 rule or charge bounds it, and a named test fails without that bound |
| partial | bounded in some shapes only, or bounded but no test fails without the bound |
| gap | not bounded |
| not-applicable | the cost cannot arise; the row says why |

Severity is the verifier's rating of the row (blocker, major, minor, none); the five rows the completeness check reclassified (VLU-41, CMP-58, VAL-13, CX-28, VAL-68; section 6.2) carry the severity of the CC row with the same consequence, and their evidence records the original status and severity. A class takes the highest severity of its members. Under the quality standard (AGENTS.md, user decision 16) every partial and gap row must close whatever its severity; severity orders the work.

## 2. Inventory

Under draft 2020-12 the library's objCompiler.compile (objcompiler.go:19-84) reads $id and $anchor, then runs compileDraft4, compileDraft6, compileDraft7, compileDraft2019 and compileDraft2020 in turn, because each layer runs when DraftVersion is at least its draft. So every keyword of every earlier layer is acted on unless its layer guards it by version or vocabulary. The resource collection (roots.go:81-191) and the meta-schema check (roots.go:237-245) read further keywords at every collected position. The tables list each keyword and mechanism with the matrix rows that cover it.

### 2.1 Keywords by compile layer

Line numbers are objcompiler.go unless named. WP-51 sets neither AssertFormat nor AssertContent (schema.go:261-265). A keyword "only with AssertContent" is therefore never acted on. A keyword "only when assertFormat" is acted on wherever the resource's dialect has the format-assertion vocabulary (:436-445): never at walked positions, where the walk accepts only the exact draft 2020-12 $schema, but on library-only routes, where a resource's $schema can give it that vocabulary (CC-01 to CC-04, GC-21).

| Keyword | Layer and library code | What the library does under draft 2020-12 | Rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| $id | entry, :21 (draft.getID, draft.go:165-179) | Stored as the schema ID; at collection it becomes the resource base (roots.go:103-147). | CMP-11, CMP-12, CMP-43, CMP-44, CMP-66, CMP-67, REFS-09, REFS-24, REFS-36, REFS-37, REFS-43, VLU-54 |
| $anchor | entry, :40 | Stored as s.Anchor; collected per resource (root.go:118-123). | CMP-45, CMP-46, REFS-05, REFS-38, REFS-39 |
| $ref | draft 4, :88-97 enqueueRef (core vocabulary) | Resolved against the resource base (enqueueRefVal :361-384: url.join, root.resolve, the loader for other roots) and the target enqueued; sibling keywords still apply. | CX-01, REFS-23, REFS-26, REFS-27, VAL-14, CMP-39, CMP-42, CMP-64, CMP-65 |
| allOf, anyOf, oneOf | draft 4, :100-102 enqueueArr | Each item enqueued. | CMP-49, VAL-39, CX-07, CX-08, CX-09 |

| items (array form), additionalItems | draft 4, :105-114, only when DraftVersion < 2020 | Not compiled as keywords under 2020-12. additionalItems is still a collected subschema path (draft.go:43), so a reference, anchor or $id below it makes the library compile it without its own meta-check. | CMP-26, CMP-27, REFS-40, REFS-41, VAL-30, CX-23 |

## 3. The matrix

One table per area, every verified row. Columns: ID; feature; cost dimension; status; severity; the WP-51 rule or charge that bounds it (file:line, or none); the tests that prove it; the verifier's gap fix for the row; and the gap class (section 4) for partial and gap rows. Below each table, the library path and the evidence of every row.

### 3.1 Compile (CMP)

79 rows: 15 bounded, 26 partial, 35 gap, 3 not applicable. Library files: objcompiler.go, draft.go, compiler.go, vocab.go, content.go, metaschemas/.

| ID | Feature | Cost | Status | Sev. | WP-51 bound | Tests | Gap fix | Class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CMP-01 | Compile work queue: count of queued schemas (enqueue, linear queue.get scan, c.schemas lookup) | C1 | bounded | none | walk.go:249-253 MaxSubschemas (schema.go:39, 4,096 positions) | TestSubschemaBound, TestCompileScale (time measured by BenchmarkCompileSchemaWorstCase) | - | - |
| CMP-02 | Compile work queue x location bytes: long member names in properties, $defs, definitions, dependentSchemas, dependencies and patternProperties maps (queue.get compares equal-length locations in full) | C1 | gap | blocker | none: MaxSchemaBytes bounds the document, MaxSubschemas the count, nothing bounds the bytes of a location | none | The auditor's budget (location bytes summed over schema positions) covers only this member. The class is 'string bytes x multiplicity', and its other members multiply by other counts, which that sum does not see: every value the meta-schema or the walk visits, schema or not (CMP-05, CMP-72 to CMP-76), references to a long target pointer (CMP-64/65), the base URI per reference (CMP-11/12) and per nested resource (CMP-66/67), index aliases (CMP-06), and the bookkeeping copies (CMP-09). Fix the class with one walk-time budget (refused with RZ-CFG-005 at the member or reference that crosses it), charged wherever a pointer or URL is built, before anything builds it: (a) each value of the document, schema or not, the length of its pointer as the library spells it (escape, then PathEscape per token); (b) each schema position, its location times the queued entries of equal length the library compares it with, or a cap on location bytes per position; (c) each reference, base URI + value + resolved target pointer; (d) each resource, its resolved $id length times (resources + positions), for the library's resource scans; (e) the CMP-06 canonical-index rule. Also stop WP-51 building pointers it never reports (CMP-72 to CMP-75). Tests, one per member and per keyword (properties, $defs, definitions, dependentSchemas, dependencies, patternProperties), with ASCII, '/', '~' and non-ASCII names, each refused in milliseconds through CompileSchema, Check and Factory.Build with a compileAlloc bound, and the largest accepted shape of each member added to analysisCases. | GC-03 |
| CMP-03 | Compile work queue x location bytes (long member names): compile peak memory | C2 | gap | blocker | none | none | The CMP-02 class budget, charge (b). Add a compileAlloc assertion for the refused shape and the largest accepted one. | GC-03 |
| CMP-04 | Location strings kept by every compiled Schema (and c.schemas keys) for long member names | C3 | gap | major | none; TestPatternProgramMemory bounds only positions x about 2.4 KB and patterns | none | The CMP-02 class budget, charge (b). Add a kept-memory case (TestPatternProgramMemory style) at the largest accepted location budget, and restate doc.go:114-121. | GC-03 |
| CMP-05 | The library's meta-check builds the instance pointer of every value it applies the meta root ('#meta') to (metaResource via handleMeta); the compile re-derives pointers per position (root.resource prefix walk, urlPtr.lookup, location strings) | C1 | gap | blocker | none. #### Library paths and evidence (CMP)

- **CMP-01.** Library: compiler.go:153-164 enqueue (c.schemas map lookup, then queue.get), compiler.go:297-303 queue.get (slices.IndexFunc comparing urlPtr), compiler.go:190-214 doCompile: quadratic in the schemas one Compile queues. Evidence: Verified: TestSubschemaBound asserts the finding at MaxSubschemas and through a hidden reference; TestCompileScale asserts one finding at 2 x MaxSubschemas. Auditor control: 4,000 one-byte-named leaves compile in 0.26 s and allocate 62 MB. The count is bounded; the bytes compared per entry are not (CMP-02).
- **CMP-02.** Library: compiler.go:297-303 queue.get compares sch.up == up (url + ptr strings) against every queued entry; util.go:99-105 append/append2 (fmt.Sprintf per child pointer); schema.go(lib):279-281 newSchema Location = up.String() (util.go:250-252, percent-encoded by encode util.go:120-129). Evidence: r9 blocker 2, re-confirmed by the auditor (5,000-byte name over 4,000 leaves: properties 5.7 s, $defs 6.3 s, dependentSchemas 6.6 s; 20,000-byte name 27.2 s, 1.37 GB). Verifier re-run, \<v>/probe.test -test.run TestVerMisc (\<v> = /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-ruralz/0aabfe6e-34a6-51d2-9b9b-23886b703760/scratchpad/wp51audit/compile-verify, built with CGO_ENABLED=0 go test -c -overlay \<v>/overlay.json): a 48,764-byte schema, one 5,000-byte properties name over 4,000 leaves, compiles in 7.0 s and allocates 395 MB, accepted, at a load of about 1.
- **CMP-03.** Library: compiler.go:153-164, util.go:99-105, util.go:250-252 urlPtr.String, roots.go:166-188 per-position collect maps. Evidence: Auditor probe TestAuditLongNames: 404 MB for a 5,000-byte name over 4,000 leaves, 1,371 MB for a 20,000-byte name; r9: 4.1 GB for a 200,000-byte name over 1,000 leaves. Verifier re-run: 395 MB (TestVerMisc).
- **CMP-04.** Library: schema.go(lib):279-281 newSchema keeps Location; compiler.go:210-212 c.schemas[urlPtr]. Evidence: r9 measured 162-170 MB kept for a 20,000-byte name over 4,000 leaves and 412 MB for a 200,000-byte name over 1,000 leaves. Not re-measured; the same documents still compile (CMP-02), so the kept memory is unchanged.
- **CMP-05.** Library: validator.go:730-740 metaResource (ptr += '/' then += escape(tok): a new string per token) called from handleMeta validator.go:742-750 in validateSelf, validateVal and validateValue (659-728) for every value the meta-schema's $dynamicRef '#meta' reaches: every item of allOf, anyOf, oneOf and prefixItems, and every member of properties, $defs, definitions, dependentSchemas, dependencies and patternProperties, schema or not; root.go:20-32 resource() hashes every pointer prefix; util.go:221-244 lookup per queued schema. Evidence: The auditor rated this major and assumed the cost was positions x name length. Verifier probe TestVerAllOfLeavesProfile, {'$defs':{'<100,000 x a>':{'allOf':[0 x 4,000]}}} (108 KB): 3.2 s, 5.1 GB, refused. CPU profile: metaResource 17.6%, the library's collect (CMP-76) 27%, WP-51 metaIssues (CMP-74) 20%, WP-51 eachSubschema (CMP-72) 10%. With 60,000 items (220 KB, TestVerNonSchemaMembers): 2 min 17 s and 76.9 GB allocated before the refusal, against 2 schema positions. The multiplier is the number of values the meta-schema applies #meta to, not the position count, so a budget over positions does not bound it.
- **CMP-06.** Library: util.go:221-244 urlPtr.lookup uses strconv.Atoi (:234), which accepts a sign and leading zeros; roots.go:193-214 ensureSubschema: an unprocessed pointer triggers clone, addSubschema and a full meta-schema check of the subtree; compiler.go:153-164 a distinct urlPtr is a new Schema. Evidence: Auditor: n=450 aliases of a 75,000-item enum (194,543 B): 22.3 s, 10.7 GB, accepted. Verifier re-run (TestVerMisc): 100 aliases of the same enum (154,001 B): 4.5 s, 1.93 GB, accepted with 0 issues.
- **CMP-07.** Library: roots.go:205-213 r.clone() per alias; validator.go:684-705 validateVal per enum or examples item per alias; walk.go:276-278 checkNumbers allocates one pointer string per element per alias. Evidence: Auditor: 10.7 GB at n=450 (enum), 7.2 GB (examples), 4.4 GB (required, n=300). Verifier: 1.93 GB at n=100.
- **CMP-08.** Library: util.go:234 strconv.Atoi; an alias target's Location keeps the alias spelling. Evidence: Re-run of the auditor's TestAuditLegacyAndAnnotations: {'$ref':'#/allOf/00','allOf':[{'type':'string'}]} compiles, and body 1 is refused at '/allOf/00/type' (likewise '/allOf/-0/type' and '/allOf/+0/type').
- **CMP-09.** Library: roots.go:193-214 ensureSubschema -> root.go:137-152 clone (re-inserts every subschemasProcessed key, hashing each pointer). Evidence: The entry count is bounded and tested: 125 targets over 3,800 properties pass and 130 are refused at the crossing reference. The bytes per entry are not bounded. Each copy re-hashes every pointer key, so a copy costs the document's location bytes, not about 0.14 us per entry. Under a long ancestor name (CMP-02) the copies multiply those bytes up to maxCheckCopies times (code reasoning; CMP-02's queue cost dominates such a shape).
- **CMP-10.** Library: roots.go:193-214 ensureSubschema, roots.go:237-245 validate (the whole keyword tree of an unprocessed target). Evidence: Bounded and tested for repeats of the same pointer string. Index aliases repeat the whole check under a new spelling and escape both bounds (CMP-06: 100 aliases, 4.5 s, 1.93 GB). So the class is bounded in some shapes only.
- **CMP-11.** Library: objcompiler.go:361-384 enqueueRefVal -> util.go:27-49 url.join (gourl.Parse(base), ResolveReference, String, per reference); root.go:58-76 resolve compares resource ids. Evidence: Auditor: a 120 KB $id with 3,800 references (222 KB) takes 14.4 s and 4.28 GB, accepted. Verifier re-run (TestVerMisc): a 60 KB $id with 2,000 references (113,788 B) takes 3.8 s and 1.22 GB, accepted.
- **CMP-12.** Library: util.go:27-49 (resolved.String() allocates the whole URL per reference). Evidence: Auditor: 4.28 GB (L=120,000, 3,800 refs), 3.28 GB (L=180,000, 2,000 refs). Verifier: 1.22 GB (L=60,000, 2,000 refs).
- **CMP-13.** Library: roots.go:126-135 (duplicate scan per new resource), roots.go:150-159 (baseRes scan per position), root.go:58-76 resolve per reference, roots.go:166-188 collect maps per position. Evidence: Auditor: 4,000 $id resources 0.48 s; 2,000 ids + 2,000 refs 0.51 s. Verifier: 4,000 nested ids under a short base, 0.53 s, 56 MB (TestVerNestedIDs, L=0). The same scans compare whole ids, so with a long base they turn into CMP-66 (13 s).
- **CMP-14.** Library: compiler.go:223-236 compileValue: for every anchor, slices.Contains over res.dynamicAnchors, then enqueue. Evidence: Auditor: 4,000 $dynamicAnchor members compile in 0.375 s (53 MB). Bounded by the position count but untested; names are bounded by MaxSchemaBytes, so A x D x name length stays below about 0.3 G byte comparisons.
- **CMP-15.** Library: compiler.go:178-214 Compile/doCompile per call (a position already compiled is a c.schemas hit with an empty queue). Evidence: Deliberately stricter than the library: the bounds also apply to positions the library would never compile. Documented and tested; the cost is bounded by MaxSubschemas.
- **CMP-16.** Library: n/a (WP-51 entry, before the library). Evidence: Auditor: a 63 MB raw document of empty objects takes 18.0 s and 3.1 GB before the size refusal. Verifier re-run, TestVerPrepareRaw: a 12 MB document of 4,000,000 empty objects takes 1.73 s and 543 MB, then is refused 'larger than 262144 bytes'.
- **CMP-17.** Library: n/a. Evidence: Auditor: 3,144 MB before the refusal (63 MB raw). Verifier: 543 MB at 12 MB raw.
- **CMP-18.** Library: roots.go:41-60 addRoot -> roots.go:237-245 validate; draft.go:266-305 getSchema returns Draft2020.sch; validator.go:742-750 handleMeta. Evidence: Bounded per position. Escapes: index aliases repeat it (CMP-06), and its per-value pointer work is unbounded (CMP-05). A target the base mismatch makes the library compile below additionalItems is never checked (CMP-69/70).
- **CMP-19.** Library: objcompiler.go:149-179, 277-278, 485-505 numVal/intVal (big.Rat.SetString(fmt.Sprint(v))); validator.go:515-552 numValidate and util.go:272-275 isInteger in the meta-check. Evidence: Verifier re-ran the auditor's mutant binaries against the compile tests (TestCompile|TestName|TestNumber|TestComparesNumbers|FuzzCompileSchema|TestCheck|TestGolden|TestAdditionalItems|TestMetaChecks|TestReferenceLadder|TestSubschemaBound|TestSizeBound|TestIssueCap|TestMetaIssues|TestInspector|TestFanOut). drop_multipleOf, drop_exclusiveMinimum and drop_minContains all pass. The rule is also bypassed where the walk does not walk: under a $recursiveRef target (CMP-33) and at a base-mismatch target (CMP-70; TestVerBaseMismatchEscape: minimum 1e999999 compiled in 45 ms and accepted).
- **CMP-20.** Library: validator.go:515-552 numValidate: num() returns nil when SetString fails, and the meta-schema's multipleOf exclusiveMinimum 0 calls nil.Cmp. Evidence: Re-run of the auditor's TestAuditMetaPanics: with the library alone, {'multipleOf':1e9999999} panics with a nil pointer dereference. The WP-51 rule prevents it at walked positions, but no test pins multipleOf, and $recursiveRef targets bypass the rule (CMP-34).
- **CMP-21.** Library: util.go:334-364 duplicates, util.go:366-405 writeHash. Evidence: Bounded and tested at walked positions. A $recursiveRef target is meta-checked by the library but never walked, so its lists are compared with math/big (CMP-34).
- **CMP-22.** Library: util.go:398-400. Evidence: Verifier probe TestVerMisc: {'$recursiveRef':'#/x','x':{'type':[21 literals e9999999]}} panics in compileRaw with a nil pointer dereference. Every walked position is covered.
- **CMP-23.** Library: validator.go:135-146 format regex -> RegexpEngine.validate (compiler.go:321-328); objcompiler.go:117-126, 167-172. Evidence: Bounded at walked positions. Unmeasured patterns compile under a $recursiveRef target (CMP-36) and at a base-mismatch target (TestVerBaseMismatchEscape: 30 x [a-z]{1000} compiled, 43 ms, 15.7 MB, accepted).
- **CMP-24.** Library: objcompiler.go:118-125, 168 (Regexp kept in Schema.Pattern/PatternProperties). Evidence: The auditor's combined probe keeps 15.93 MB within the test's 16 MiB. Unmeasured programs are kept under $recursiveRef (CMP-36) and base-mismatch targets (CMP-70).
- **CMP-25.** Library: format.go:515-560 parseURL/validateURI/validateURIReference; metaschemas/draft/2020-12/meta/core. Evidence: Linear in the string length, once per check, with no I/O (url.Parse only). It repeats with every index alias of its subtree (CMP-06). Identifier-bearing targets cannot be aliased: the walk reports a duplicate $id or anchor.
- **CMP-26.** Library: metaschemas/draft/2020-12 has no additionalItems; roots.go:166-188 collects them as processed, so ensureSubschema skips the check. Evidence: Bounded for every path the walk resolves the way the library does. Bypassed via $recursiveRef (CMP-34) and via a reference whose base the walk computes differently (CMP-69: Check finds nothing, Factory.Build succeeds, Handle panics with 'division by zero').

#### Library paths and evidence (VAL)

- **VAL-01.** Library: validator.go:75-81 validate: true returns uneval, false returns FalseSchema error. Evidence: Code reading confirms the cited lines. A false schema adds one error with no keyword path. keywordParts maps it to the schema location (validate.go:174-176). Not mutated.
- **VAL-02.** Library: validator.go:84-90, 884-896. Evidence: Mutation: removing working.go:1078 fails TestWorkCharges/string, lengths, members, number, pattern, pattern_class, enum_of_strings and others.
- **VAL-03.** Library: validator.go:84-90 RefCycle; kwLoc validator.go:898-911. Evidence: Code reading. A cycle through a dynamic $recursiveRef retarget is outside the graph; see VAL-48. No $recursiveRef cycle row exists (VAL-47).
- **VAL-04.** Library: validator.go:92-95; schema.go:129-156 typeOf; util.go:329-331, 401-403. Evidence: typeOf returns invalidType only for NaN/Inf floats or other Go types. The decoder produces neither.
- **VAL-05.** Library: validator.go:98-103; util.go:272-275. Evidence: Code reading. Types is a bitset, so the containment check is O(1). The single parse is charged, and the body literal is range-checked first.
- **VAL-06.** Library: validator.go:101; schema.go:239-251 (at most 7 names). Evidence: Code reading. The list has at most 7 entries. In the decision pass the error is empty, but the kind (with its ToStrings slice) is built before vd.error and becomes garbage at once (VAL-69).
- **VAL-07.** Library: validator.go:106-113; util.go:286-332. Evidence: Code reading. equals returns early on a length mismatch, and only the first missing body name is hashed in full. This holds only for const values the walk range-checked; for an unchecked const see VAL-49.
- **VAL-08.** Library: util.go:322-328. Evidence: Code reading plus the test rows: a nested body number outside the bounds is refused with DetailNumber.
- **VAL-09.** Library: validator.go:116-133; schema.go:264-270. Evidence: Code reading plus the pinned row. Every value is charged, with no discount for an early match. Bounded for schema literals within the number bounds; see VAL-49 for literals outside them.
- **VAL-10.** Library: validator.go:515-545. Evidence: Code reading confirms that the schema bound's digits are absent from the work model. The auditor's probe measured 0.968 ns/unit with bounds at the number limits. Not re-probed.
- **VAL-11.** Library: validator.go:527-550. Evidence: Mutation: removing working.go:1184 fails all four rows.
- **VAL-12.** Library: validator.go:547-551. Evidence: Mutation: removing working.go:1187 fails TestWorkCharges/multipleOf and two_multipleOf. At checked positions the meta-schema refuses a zero divisor; a zero divisor reached through $recursiveRef is VAL-49.
- **VAL-13.** Library: validator.go:136-146. Evidence: Code reading: s.Format stays nil, so validation never runs a format validator. checkApart's AssertFormat (schema.go:352) applies to the meta-check compiler only. Finalize (completeness check): reclassified from not-applicable, severity none, to partial, major. s.Format is set at a library-only position whose dialect has format-assertion, and validator.go:136-142 then runs the format validator on every body string evaluated there (CC-01, CC-03). The bound named holds at walked positions.
- **VAL-14.** Library: validator.go:149-157, 754-767. Evidence: Code reading; the charges are pinned in those rows. Not mutated.
- **VAL-15.** Library: validator.go:201-213, 328-340. Evidence: O(1) checks; the units are pinned.
- **VAL-16.** Library: validator.go:215-224, 850-861. Evidence: Mutation: removing lookedUpBytes from workWeight fails TestWorkCharges/long_required_name.
- **VAL-17.** Library: validator.go:226-238; objcompiler compileDraft4. Evidence: Mutation: removing the validate.go:173 mapping fails TestValidateKeywordLocation/dependencies_array and TestKeywordPointer. Removing lookedUpBytes fails both TestWorkCharges dependencies rows.
- **VAL-18.** Library: validator.go:240-276. Evidence: Code reading plus the pinned rows. Swiss maps with more than 8 entries hash the full name; the charge assumes every lookup hashes it.
- **VAL-19.** Library: validator.go:222-224, 241-244. Evidence: Code reading plus the exact-unit case.
- **VAL-20.** Library: validator.go:254-259. Evidence: Code reading. The pattern program measure belongs to the regex area.
- **VAL-21.** Library: validator.go:261-278. Evidence: Mutation: removing working.go:1299 fails TestWorkingSetCharges/additional_members.
- **VAL-22.** Library: validator.go:268-270. Evidence: Code reading.

- **VAL-44.** Library: validator.go:920-952. Evidence: Code reading. - **VAL-45.** Library: validator.go:802-813; compiler.go:218-231. Evidence: Code reading: the library's subschema positions (draft.go Draft2020 subschemas) are a subset of walk.go subschemaKeywords, and additionalItems with an identifier is walked live (walk.go:352).
- **VAL-46.** Library: validator.go:785, 807. Evidence: Code reading: the lines match round 9 finding 3 and are unchanged. The round-9 measurements stand (w3-s2-wp51-r9.json); I did not re-measure.
- **VAL-47.** Library: validator.go:795-800; objcompiler.go:268-273. Evidence: Mutation: removing RecursiveRef from keywords (working.go:439) and errorUnits (working.go:595) survives TestWorkCharges, TestWorkingSetCharges, TestInPlaceCycles, TestValidateKeywordLocation and TestEvaluationBound. No test file mentions $recursiveRef.
- **VAL-48.** Library: validator.go:769-779, 796-798. Evidence: Code reading plus the auditor's probe: a boolean $recursiveAnchor is refused at every checked position. Unchecked positions reached only through $recursiveRef are not library resource positions, so the resource stays the root. The bound rests on that reasoning and no test pins it.
- **VAL-49.** Library: validator.go:548 Quo; validator.go:527-545 Cmp; util.go:326-327 equals parses the schema literal per comparison; validator.go:457 MatchString. Evidence: Code reading: walk.go:346 is unchanged since round 9 finding 1. The library compiles any JSON Pointer target of $recursiveRef (objcompiler.go:353-384), including positions under unknown keywords. Round 9 recorded the zero-multipleOf panic through Handle. I did not re-probe.
- **VAL-50.** Library: validator.go:730-750. Evidence: Code reading.
- **VAL-51.** Library: validator.go:186-195, 818-848. Evidence: Code reading plus the pinned units.
- **VAL-52.** Library: validator.go:659-728, 880-882, 521. Evidence: Code reading. These are garbage only; the live part is charged through the stack and the errors.
- **VAL-53.** Library: validator.go:70-196, 659-728. Evidence: Code reading. The stack charge is bounded only relative to the working-set limit, which is 8 times maxRequestBodyBytes (filter.go:204, 286-291). pkg/config/v1alpha1/gateway.go:153-155 sets no maximum for that field. The Go runtime's maximum goroutine stack is fixed, and overflowing it is a fatal, unrecoverable error. With 64 levels and chains of up to 4096 per level, the admitted stack is limited only by the configured limits. I did not probe this, by design.
- **VAL-54.** Library: validator.go:171-184, 222-224, 241-244, 570-572, 605. Evidence: Code reading. Boolean mode skips work only after an error; propertyNames always runs in full mode; the wrapper resource has no anchors, so dynamic resolution is unchanged.
- **VAL-55.** Library: validator.go:227, 241, 254, 287, 309, 316, 640, 650. Evidence: Code reading: in full mode the leaf set does not depend on iteration order.
- **VAL-56.** Library: walk over ValidationError (validator.go:960-972). Evidence: Pinned in TestHandleLocationWalk (charge lower bounds and the refusal).
- **VAL-57.** Library: validate.go:124-126. Evidence: Mutation: changing stop() to false at validate.go:125 fails TestHandleLocationWalk ('context ended during the walk: ... at /$defs, want DetailMismatch alone'). The auditor's 'no test' claim is wrong.
- **VAL-58.** Library: validator.go full mode. Evidence: Pinned in TestHandleLocationPass (reservations and refusals).
- **VAL-59.** Library: kind/kind.go KeywordPath through validate.go:161-178. Evidence: The auditor's probe reported correct locations for all of these except minContains (VAL-33). Code reading confirms the default branch uses KeywordPath. Not re-probed.
- **VAL-60.** Library: validator.go takes no context. Evidence: The refusal is pinned. The per-unit calibration is checked only by BenchmarkWorkModel. Missing terms are VAL-10, VAL-46 and VAL-49.
- **VAL-61.** Library: validator.go:684-705. Evidence: Code reading.
- **VAL-62.** Library: validator.go:15-17. Evidence: Documented contract, and the test rows were read.
- **VAL-63.** Library: validator.go (no loader use). Evidence: Nothing in validator.go or util.go calls the loader.
- **VAL-64.** Library: Location strings. Evidence: Pinned.
- **VAL-65.** Library: validator.go:784-789, 884-896 walk to the outermost scope; schema.go:336 wrapper. Evidence: Code reading. Each dynamic resolution walks 3 uncharged scope steps in the decision pass (12 units against the evalUnits margin of 384 charged vs about 222 measured), so the work is covered only by that margin, with no explicit term.
- **VAL-66.** Library: validator.go:296, 685. Evidence: Code reading: grep finds no InstanceLocation use in the validation path (metaIssues uses it only for compile-time meta-schema errors).
- **VAL-67.** Library: util.go:346-363. Evidence: Code reading: equal items always share a bucket, so the validity result does not depend on the seed.
- **VAL-68.** Library: validator.go:15-51 allocates per call and never mutates the Schema. Evidence: Code reading plus the test. Finalize (completeness check): reclassified from bounded, severity none, to partial, blocker. An asserted format 'regex' at a library-only position calls WP-51's regexpCache.compile (schema.go:393-397) from every request and writes its map without a lock; 8 goroutines calling Handle end the process with 'fatal error: concurrent map writes' or 'concurrent map read and map write' (CC-01: 3 of 3 runs, and again when this document was finalized). TestHandleConcurrent's schema asserts no format, so it cannot see this.
- **VAL-69.** Library: validator.go:101, 111, 131, 528-549, 822-825. Evidence: Code reading: arguments are evaluated before vd.error, which drops the kind in boolean mode.

#### Library paths and evidence (REFS)

- **REFS-01.** Library: roots.go:216-235 addSubschema: base := r.resource(ptr) (root.go:20-32, longest pointer prefix that is a resource) for the target's resources and anchors; compiler.go:275 compileObject and objcompiler.go:361-384 enqueueRefVal resolve every reference of the target against c.res.id = that resource; roots.go:209 meta-checks the library-only target (util.go:334-405 duplicates/writeHash). Evidence: Verified on the working tree, probe TestRefsP1BaseMismatch (copied to \<scratch>/wp51audit/refsv/, build: CGO_ENABLED=0 go test -c -o \<scratch>/wp51audit/refsv/probe.test -overlay \<scratch>/wp51audit/refsv/overlay.json ./internal/filter/validation/jsonschema/, run from the package directory). The 345-byte {"$ref":"#/$defs/x/foo","zzz":{},"$defs":{"x":{"$id":"https://a.example/dir/sub","foo":{"$ref":"#/zzz"},"zzz":{"type":[1e9999999,...,21e9999999]}}}} panics CompileSchema (nil pointer dereference) in 0.56 ms. Walk view: positions ["" /$defs/x /$defs/x/foo /zzz], checkOrder [/$defs/x/foo /zzz], no issues: the walk walked the root's /zzz, the library compiles /$defs/x/zzz. Nothing recovers, so Check crashes stage H and Factory.Build crashes the Node.

### 3.4 Values (VLU)

56 rows: 29 bounded, 12 partial, 12 gap, 3 not applicable. Library files: kind/kind.go, util.go (equality, hashing, numbers), output.go, format.go.

| ID | Feature | Cost | Status | Sev. | WP-51 bound | Tests | Gap fix | Class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Tests: TestWorkingSetCharges and TestWorkingSetCoversPeak rows for maxLength 2^63, 2^64-1 and 1e19 (decision and location trees within the charge), and a Handle row at the default limit expecting 413 for the 50-branch allOf body. | GC-05 |

Tests: a compile-cost row with 20 literals of 1e999999 behind $recursiveRef refused by the walk before the library runs (assert on the walk's own refusal and on an allocation bound, not time). | GC-01 |

#### Library paths and evidence (VLU)

- **VLU-01.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:515-545 numValidate: num() parses fmt.Sprintf("%v", v) once per evaluation, ignores ok, then Cmp against s.Minimum etc.; a literal math/big refuses (exponent past about 10^6) gives nil and Cmp panics. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): parses_minimum killed (TestHandleNumberPaths, TestWorkCharges/number, TestWorkingSetCharges); parses_maximum killed only by TestValidateNumberBounds; parses_exclusiveMinimum and parses_exclusiveMaximum killed only by the charge rows TestWorkingSetCharges/bounds_that_can_all_fail and exclusive_bounds_that_meet (no test sends an out-of-range body number under an exclusive bound); deep_bound killed by TestNestingLimit; note_unique, note_constenum, note_integer killed by TestHandleNumberPaths and TestComparesNumbers. bound_unknown (drop 'a.st == nil') survived but is equivalent: the depth bound's dimParses is positive wherever any schema at that depth parses. The analysis graph follows $recursiveRef (inspect.go:52), so the body bound also holds at $recursiveRef targets.
- **VLU-02.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:515-545 (one parse per evaluation, then a big.Rat.Cmp per keyword, which multiplies each numerator by the other denominator); util.go:272-275 isInteger; util.go:322-328 equals (two parses and a Cmp); util.go:338 and 355 duplicates (pairwise and bucket equals). Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite): numwork_noexp (drop the 12-per-exponent-unit term, 12,000 of the 16,352 units numberWork charges at the bounds) SURVIVED the whole suite: no TestWorkCharges row has an exponent ('number' is 1.5) and BenchmarkWorkModel is a benchmark with no numeric-bound case. Verifier timing probes TestVVCompareCost (best of 5) and TestVVCompareMean (mean of 20, GC included), 500 to 2,000 items of the 128-byte literal -9{61}.7{59}e-1000 and e1000 under minimum, maximum, exclusiveMinimum, const, integer type, enum and pairwise uniqueItems against -1e1000, 1e1000, -1e-1000, -8{61}.7{59}e+-1000 and -0.3{119}e-1000, at load 1.0 to 1.5: 4.0 to 13.9 us per item, 0.17 to 0.64 ns per charged unit (worst: minimum against 1e1000, 0.64). The auditor's 0.93 to 1.43 ns/unit (16 to 24 us per item) was not reproduced on the quieter machine, so the comparison product does not break the 0.8 ns/unit claim at the current bounds; what is unproven is the exponent term itself: with it removed, 1e-1000 literals would run at about 7 to 10 ns per charged unit and no test would fail.
- **VLU-03.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:546-551 numValidate: new(big.Rat).Quo(num(), s.MultipleOf) then IsInt; objcompiler.go:149 numVal for the divisor. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite): quo_zero (quoWork returns 0) and divisor_zero (no divisorWork) both killed by TestWorkCharges/multipleOf and two_multipleOf; parses_multipleOf killed by TestMultipleOfCounts. Auditor: BenchmarkWorkModel 'multipleOf 3e1000, long literals' 0.37 ns/unit; 2,000 128-byte literals under multipleOf 0.3{100}e-990 at 0.26 ns/unit. At $recursiveRef targets the divisor is charged from its compiled digits (verifier probe: multipleOf 1e-99999 at 0.2 ns/unit).
- **VLU-04.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:548 Quo panics on a zero divisor; meta/validation multipleOf {type number, exclusiveMinimum 0} checked by roots.go:237-245 at every compiled position; objcompiler.go:149. Evidence: Auditor probe: {"$recursiveRef":"#/x","x":{"multipleOf":0}} is refused by the library's ensureSubschema meta-check, so the $recursiveRef bypass does not reach Quo with 0. A huge multipleOf behind $recursiveRef does panic, in the meta-check itself, before Quo (VLU-51).
- **VLU-05.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 objcompiler.go:150-163: if c.boolean("exclusiveMaximum") { s.ExclusiveMaximum = s.Maximum; s.Maximum = nil } (same for minimum), run for every draft because compileDraft4 is cumulative. Evidence: Auditor probe TestVDraft4Exclusive: {maximum:5, exclusiveMaximum:true}, {minimum:5, exclusiveMinimum:true}, {maximum:5, exclusiveMaximum:false} and the same below a referenced additionalItems are refused ('got boolean, want number'). No test pins it, so a regression in checkApart or the meta-check would silently turn maximum into exclusiveMaximum.
- **VLU-06.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:99 (Types.contains(integer) && t==number && isInteger(v)); util.go:272-275 isInteger. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): parses_integer killed by TestHandleNumberPaths. Auditor probe TestVLongLiteralWork: 2,000 128-byte literals under {items:{type:integer}} 1.01 ns/unit; verifier probe 0.22 to 0.38 ns/unit. The exponent term of the charge is untested (VLU-02, numwork_noexp survived).
- **VLU-07.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:99 s.Types.contains(t) || (...isInteger(v)): with number listed, isInteger is never called. Evidence: Verifier probe TestVVNoParse (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/zz_vv_test.go): {"type":["integer","number"]} and ["number","integer"] with body 1e1001 -> Handle 400 DetailNumber while the library validates it as valid without parsing; {"type":"number"} passes. 07 req 80 and user decision 14 refuse only numbers the library would parse.
- **VLU-08.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:105-133 const/enum; util.go:322-328 equals: SetString both sides, false (no panic) when either fails; validator.go:117 enum gate on types. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): parses_const SURVIVED the whole suite (confirms the auditor); parses_enum killed by TestHandleNumberPaths and TestWorkCharges/enum_of_numbers. With the const branch gone, {items:{const:1}} lets 1e999999 body literals through the bound uncharged, about 45 ms of uncancellable math/big each (doc.go:613-615).
- **VLU-09.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:286-321 equals recursion (length check, lookup of each body key in the const map, element-wise for arrays), nested numbers at util.go:322-328. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): bound_comparing killed by TestHandleNumberPaths; nested_maxwork killed by TestWorkCharges/nested_const_number; const_objects killed by three TestWorkCharges rows. equals stops at a length mismatch and the first missing key, so a body number is parsed at most once per schema number it is paired with. (The DetailNumber over-refusal for composites whose shape differs is VLU-11.)
- **VLU-10.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:342-350; util.go:334-364 duplicates (pairwise equals up to 20 items, hash map past it); util.go:396-400 writeHash SetString then num.Num() with no ok check -> nil pointer panic past about a 10^6 exponent. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): bound_hash (drop 'c[dimHash] > 0') and every_parses (every node dimParses 0) each SURVIVED the suite, because each condition alone still applies the bound; removing both lets the TestHandleNumberPaths row through. Auditor probe: a 31-item uniqueItems array of 1e1001 answers DetailNumber without panicking (hash path). The only test row is a one-item array the library never parses (VLU-11).
- **VLU-11.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:343 (len(arr) > 1 guard); util.go:290 and 305 equals returns at a length mismatch before any nested parse; util.go:322-324 returns false without parsing when the other side is not a number. Evidence: Verifier probe TestVVNoParse: under uniqueItems [1e1001], [1e1001,"a"], [[1e1001],["a"]] are 400 DetailNumber while the library validates each as valid without parsing ([1e1001,1] is parsed, correctly refused). {"const":{"a":1}} with {"a":1e1001,"b":1} and {"const":[1,2]} with [1e1001] answer DetailNumber where the library fails at /const without parsing (detail of 07 req 81 replaced by req 80's). Same for enum composites.
- **VLU-12.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:346-362 duplicates compares an item with the earlier items of its hash bucket; util.go:396-400 hashes Num().Bytes() then Denom().Bytes(). Evidence: Verifier probe TestVVUniqueDecimals at the 10 MiB default: 2,000 distinct \<i>.5e-300 (22,894 B, 0.22% of the limit) charged 2,981,816,984 units (limit 2,684,354,560) -> Handle 413 errWorkTooLarge, while the library validates it (valid) in 4.1 ms (0.001 ns per charged unit); 500 items pass (737,842,100 units). \<i>.5e-30 (2,000 items) passes at 132,783,896 units for 2.7 ms. Matches the auditor (408 times over the real cost).
- **VLU-13.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:396-400 writeHash bytes of numerator then denominator without sign or separator. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): smallrat_expsign (smallRat ignores the exponent's sign, so 2E-4 is taken as 2e4) SURVIVED the suite; colliders_one (always 1) and integers_zero killed by TestUniqueItemsComparisons. The auditor's coverage: the m==0 branch and the exponent shift loops of smallRat and hashColliders' part-past-64-bits split never run. A wrong p/q undercounts colliders for exponent literals.
- **VLU-14.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:334-364 duplicates (map[uint64][]int, per-call random maphash seed), util.go:366-405 writeHash (type tag byte, strings whole, arrays/objects without length delimiters), util.go:286-321 equals. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): hash_numbers (drop the a.hash x numberWork term) killed by TestHandleFitEdges/uniqueItems_objects_of_a_number; integers_zero and colliders_one killed by TestUniqueItemsComparisons. Code reasoning: the first byte of every hash is a type tag, so only same-kind items share buckets; each pair is compared once and each item's bucket holds at most comparisons()+1 items, so charging each value comparisons() times its own compare cost covers both sides. The hash pass's own parse of each number is VLU-56.
- **VLU-15.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 objcompiler.go:485-497 numVal (SetString; a refused literal silently drops the keyword), 499-505 intVal; roots.go:237-245 meta-check -> validator.go:99 isInteger and 515-545 numValidate on keyword values (nil Rat Cmp panic for multipleOf via exclusiveMinimum 0). Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): removing one keyword from parsedKeywords: walk_multipleOf, walk_exclusiveMinimum, walk_minContains, walk_maxLength, walk_minItems, walk_maxProperties all SURVIVED the whole suite; walk_const killed (TestCompileSchemaFindings/number_in_const_data). The auditor also found exclusiveMaximum, maxContains and minProperties untested and that without multipleOf CompileSchema({"multipleOf":1e9999999}) panics (stage H crash). So 9 of the 15 keywords have no test.
- **VLU-16.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 objcompiler.go:268-273 compileDraft2019 enqueueRef("$recursiveRef") under 2020-12; util.go:322-328 equals parses the unbounded schema literal at every comparison; validator.go:515-545 Cmp against an unbounded compiled bound. Evidence: Verifier probe TestVVRecursiveRefRuntime (zz_vv4_test.go), 50 items: {items:{$recursiveRef:#/x}, x:{const:[1e99999]}} 45.8 ms charged 1,540,592 units (29.7 ns/unit); x:{enum:[1e99999]} 44.5 ms, 58.2 ns/unit; x:{minimum:1e99999} 13.5 ns/unit; x:{multipleOf:1e-99999} 0.2 ns/unit. At 58 ns/unit the 2.68e9-unit limit allows about 156 s of uncancellable CPU per request; with exponents near 10^6 each comparison takes about 45 ms. Same class as round-9 blocker 1.
- **VLU-17.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 objcompiler.go:499-505 intVal: int(n.Num().Int64()) keeps the low 64 bits: 2^64 and 1e400 -> 0, 2^63 and 1e19 -> negative, 2^64+1 -> 1. Evidence: Verifier probe TestVVIntWrap: maxLength 18446744073709551616, 9223372036854775808, 1e19 and 1e400 refuse "abc" at /maxLength; minLength 18446744073709551617 and 1e400 accept "a"; maxItems 2^63 refuses [1]; minItems 1e400 accepts []; maxProperties 2^64 refuses {a:1}; minProperties 1e400 accepts {}; maxContains 2^64 refuses [1] under contains true; minContains 1e400 accepts. The meta-check accepts all of them (nonNegativeInteger). The memory consequence is VLU-50.
- **VLU-18.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 objcompiler.go:149-181 numVal/intVal per keyword; roots.go:237-245 meta-check parses integer keywords twice (type integer, minimum 0); schema.go (lib) keeps a \*big.Rat per numeric keyword. Evidence: Auditor probe TestVCompileNumbers (not re-run by the verifier): 2,025 positions of six integer keywords at 1e1000: 0.32-0.36 s, 134-142 MB allocated; 2,158 positions of five bounds at 1e-1000: 0.20-0.25 s, 68-75 MB allocated, 9.7 MB kept (4.5 KB per position, above doc.go:117's 2.4 KB, so about 18 MB at MaxSubschemas against doc.go's 16 MB); these add to doc.go's combined worst case. Bounded in size by the literal bounds; the figures are unpinned.
- **VLU-19.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:527-550 kind.Minimum{Got: num(), Want: s.Minimum} etc.; kind/kind.go:531-612. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): rat_locate killed by TestWorkingSetCoversPeak/numbers and small_numbers and four TestWorkingSetCharges rows; ratcost_flat killed by TestWorkingSetCharges. num() is cached per evaluation, so one rational per failing evaluation is kept.
- **VLU-20.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:273, 326-327, 398; validator.go:521, 548. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite): range_exponent (exponent bound 9,999) killed by TestHandleMatrix/number_out_of_range, TestHandleNumberPaths, TestGoldenCheckDiagnostics and others; range_bytes (4x the byte bound) killed by TestHandleMatrix/long_number, TestNumberInRange, TestCompileSchemaFindings/long_number. Each parse allocates at most a few KB of garbage; the 'numbers' case of TestWorkingSetCoversPeak stays within the charge.
- **VLU-21.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:198-213, 328-340, 526-545 each bound adds an error. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite): bounds_failing (lower and upper bounds never fail together) killed by TestWorkingSetCoversPeak/numbers and TestWorkingSetCharges bounds rows; pair_failing killed by TestWorkingSetCharges/member_counts_that_can_both_fail. The model reads the same compiled ints and rationals as the library, so it stays consistent for wrapped integers too, except the string length units (VLU-50).
- **VLU-22.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:272-275, 322-328; validator.go:515-552. Evidence: Code reasoning: every RFC 8259 literal is accepted by big.Rat.SetString; comparisons are exact rationals; TestValidateExactNumbers fails if numbers became float64 (9007199254740993 vs maximum 9007199254740992).
- **VLU-23.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 schema.go (lib) 129-155 typeOf -> invalidType; validator.go:92-95 kind.InvalidJsonValue at the root; util.go:330 equals default; validator.go:521 and util.go:398 SetString of arbitrary json.Number text ("abc", "1e", "", "Inf" -> nil -> panic; "0x10", "1/3" -> accepted as numbers). Evidence: Verifier probe TestVVOutsideModel: under false and under {type:string}, ValidateWithin(NaN), (+Inf), ([]string{"x"}), (map[string]string), (struct{}) return ok=true; under {minimum:0} json.Number "abc", "1e", "" and "Inf" panic with a nil pointer dereference, nested ones under {items:{minimum:0}} too; json.Number("0x10") passes {type:integer} and "1/3" passes {minimum:0}; float64 1.5 and int 3 are validated with their parses uncharged. Handle is safe (jsonval decodes strictly); the M2 callers (AI tool inputs, OpenAPI examples) are not.
- **VLU-24.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 every library parse site: validator.go:99 (isInteger), 105-133 (const/enum equals), 342-350 (uniqueItems), 515-552 (numeric keywords). Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite, 60 s each): note_unique, note_constenum, note_integer killed (TestHandleNumberPaths, TestComparesNumbers); deep_bound killed (TestNestingLimit); bound_unknown survived as an equivalent mutant (VLU-01). grep of the library for big.Rat: only util.go isInteger/equals/writeHash, validator.go numValidate and objcompiler.go numVal parse numbers.
- **VLU-25.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:469 decoded := []byte(str) at every string evaluation (drafts other than 6); validator.go:436-439 utf8.RuneCount([]byte(str)). - **VLU-26.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:438 utf8.RuneCount. Evidence: internal/jsonval/errors.go:19-20 ErrSurrogate; doc.go:259-266.
- **VLU-27.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:319-321 equals for strings: length check, then memequal only for equal lengths. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite): const_string_bytes (drop the len/compareBytes term of compareWork) SURVIVED the whole suite: the only rows ('enum of strings' with 2-byte values) charge 0 for the bytes. Go's string == compares bytes only for equal lengths and stops at the first difference, so the term is load-bearing for an enum of long strings sharing a prefix with an equal-length body string (each comparison scans the prefix while only compareUnits would be charged).
- **VLU-28.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 validator.go:241-276 (s.Properties[pname], delete(uneval.props)), 920-952 unevalFrom/merge, util.go:293-301 equals map lookups. Every per-byte hashing site of the library is charged (round-9 major fix).
- **VLU-29.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:368-378 writeHash: props slice, slices.Sort (byte-wise comparisons), each name written and v[prop] looked up. Evidence: Verifier mutants (scratch wp51audit/verify-values/mut/\<name>/, go test -overlay, full CGO_ENABLED=0 package suite): sort_units (drop sortUnits x n x bits(n)) SURVIVED the whole suite; the per-byte name terms are pinned by TestWorkCharges 'uniqueItems objects' (name_hashes killed). The count term is covered today by the margin of hashUnits (384) per member value, which every value below the array pays, so the shortfall is not measurable, but the term has no test.
- **VLU-30.** Library: jsonschema/v6@v6.0.3 util.go:95-105 jsonPointer.concat/append/append2 (fmt.Sprintf of the whole prefix per subschema), util.go:55-59 escape, util.go:119-129 encode (PathEscape), util.go:246-252 urlPtr.format/String (Schema.Location), util.go:221-244 urlPtr.lookup (strings.Split of the whole pointer per compiled position), compiler.go:153-163 enqueue (map hash of the urlPtr) and 297-303 queue.get (compares whole urlPtr values), validator.go:730-740 metaResource (ptr += per token at every meta-check step). Evidence: Verifier probe TestVVLongNames (zz_vv4_test.go), {properties:{\<name>:{allOf:[1,000 x {}]}}}: a 10,000-byte ASCII name (13 KB doc) compiles in 378 to 438 ms, allocating 167 MB and keeping 20 MB; 5,000 x e-acute (13 KB) 586 ms, 407 MB, 41 MB kept; 5,000 slashes (8 KB) 510 ms, 194 MB, 20 MB kept. A 50,000-byte name over 2,000 members (56 KB doc) took 5.3 s and allocated 1.77 GB before the fan-out finding refused it. Matches the auditor (refused 111 KB document: 1.3 s, 1.83 GB) and round-9 blocker 2.

58 rows: 23 bounded, 23 partial, 12 gap, 0 not applicable. Library files: Go regexp and regexp/syntax as the library uses them, plus interactions across all files.

| CR-01 | Regex parse: POSIX named-class lookahead inside a bracket class ('[:' with no later ':]'), parsed 4 times per pattern | C1 | gap | blocker | none. patternsize.go:269-298 scanPattern charges only Unicode escape tables and fold work; its POSIX case (patternsize.go:384, guarded by i+2 <= l.named, the LAST ':]' of expr) skips exactly the costly shape | none | Class: parser work that is not linear in the pattern and that the lexical pass does not count; the only superlinear lookahead is parseNamedClass (named capture '>', \p{...} '}', \x{...}, \Q...\E and {m,n} either consume what they scan or fail). Fix in lexer.class: for every '[:' inside a class with no later ':]', charge the bytes Index scans (len(expr)-i, O(1) per occurrence) as parse work at a measured bytes-per-instruction rate times the four parses, and refuse unparsed past MaxPatternInstructions/MaxDocumentInstructions as escapes are (so the refused shape costs no parse).
